    -   enable: loseFrameSwitch
    -   bit\_rate\_threshold: loseFrameThreshold
    -   frame\_gap: MaximumAllowedContinuousloseFrameFramenumber. 
    -   mode: loseFrameStrategyMode

-   This interface is an advanced interface, Users can selectively call, SystemHasDefault value, DefaultInstantaneousBitrateExceedsThresholdwhen as loseFrame. 
-   thisInterfaceProvidesInstantaneousBitrateExceedsThresholdwhenTwo TypesProcessingMethod: loseFrameandEncodingp\_skipFrame, Mjpegonly supportsloseFrameProcessingMethod. 
-   frame\_gapControlWhetherEvenloseFrameOrEvenEncodingp\_skipFrame. As shown in [Figure 1](#fig9874105285115).

**Figure 1**  loseFrameSchematic Diagram<a name="fig9874105285115"></a>  
![](figures/丢帧示意图.png "丢帧示意图")
-   This interface is Encoding Channelafter creation, Encoding Channelset before destruction. this interfaceEncodingDuringCallswhen, etc.toNextFramewhentakes effect. IfgopModeSwitchesafterthis interface, Needetc.toGOPtakes effectafterthis interfacetakes effect. 
-   p\_skipFrameOnly supportedGOPmode isOT\_VENC\_GOP\_MODE\_NORMAL\_PEncoding Channel. 
-   SwitchesGOPModeafterloseFrameSwitchwillDefaultClose, Such asNeedContinueUsesthis interfaceNeedRe-Configure . 
-   Such asCurrentFrameosdEnabledAndosdHasUpdates, CannotEncoding as p\_skipFrame. 
-   Encodingp\_skipFrameAndPFrameIsliceFunctionMutually Exclusive. 
-   WhenSetH.265ProtocolChannelEncodingp\_skipFramewhen, thisModuleInternalwillCloseSAOFiltering, 故RecommendedCustomerCalls[ss\_mpi\_venc\_set\_h265\_sao](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002441658333)CloseSAOFunction. 
-   Ifframe\_gapEqual to0when, IndicatesInstantaneousBitrateExceedsThresholdAllowedAlwaysloseFrame, UntilInstantaneousBitratenotGreater ThanThreshold. 
-   EnabledjumpFrameReferenceOrsvc-tEncoding, baseLayerFrameCannotEncoding as p\_skipFrame. 
-   thisInterfaceOnly supportedH.264/H.265/Mjpeg CBR/VBRRate Control mode, H.264/H.265 AVBR/QVBR/CVBRRate Control modeParameterSet.

None.

-   This interface is set after the encoding channel is created and before it is destroyed. When called during encoding, it takes effect on the next frame.

-   TheFunctionMainlybyas followsfiveParameterDetermines.
    -   enable: skip倾towardEnabledControl.
    -   gainandoffsetIsForegroundDetectionControlParameter, Used forGenerateForegroundDetectionThreshold.ThresholdCalculatesas follows: \(\(gain \* madi + 8\) \>\> 4\) + offset, where madi as CurrentBlockPixelAverage绝toError\(FirstCalculatesCurrentBlockPixelAllvalue, ThenCalculatesCurrentBlockHasPixelAndTheAllvalue绝toAllvalue\).MotionDetectionMethod: UsingMotionEstimateResultResidualSAD, 跟thisThresholdPerformsComparerelatively, IfGreater ThanthisThresholdThen认 as IsForegroundPartial.ThereforeRegardingsameoneScenario, Thresholdsmall, DetectionexittoForegroundRegionlarge, ConverselyLikewise.
    -   bg\_cost/fg\_costRespectivelySetBackgroundandForegroundskip倾towardSize.DefaultnotAdjust倾toward, , i.e., costvalue as 10.

-   Only supported for H.265/H.264 encoding channels.

None.

**Related topics**

</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

**Requirements**

-   Header: ot\_common\_venc.h, ss\_mpi\_venc.h, ot\_comm\_vb.h
-   Library: libss\_mpi.a

**Note**

-   The channel must have been created; otherwise, an error code for non-existent channel is returned.
-   This interface is Encoding Channelafter creation, Encoding Channelset before destruction.
-   This interface is EncodingDuringCallswhen, etc.toNextIFramewhentakes effect.
-   It is recommended to call this interface after creating the channel and before starting encoding to minimize calls during encoding.
-   It is recommended to call this interfaceBefore, first call [ss\_mpi\_venc\_get\_hierarchical\_qp](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408099118)Interface, before setting.
-   OnlyGOPType as NORMAL\_P, SMART\_P, ADV\_SMART\_PwhenSupportedthis interface.
-   Only supported for H.265/H.264 encoding channels.
-   RecommendedHeightFramerateMotionScenariowhenUsesTheFunction, , i.e., SceneModeset to OT\_VENC\_SCENE\_1.
-   RecommendedGOPmode isOT\_VENC\_GOP\_MODE\_NORMAL\_PandOT\_VENC\_GOP\_MODE\_SMART\_PandOT\_VENC\_GOP\_MODE\_ADV\_SMART\_PwhenUsesTheFunction.
-   TheFunctionMainlybyas followsParameterDetermines.
    -   enable: LayerqpFunctionEnabledConfigure .
    -   qp\_delta: SetEachoneLayerFrameRegarding0LayerpFrameqp\_delta.Due to0LayerpFrameqpnotNeedadjust, 故qp\_delta\[0\] as 1Layer, qp\_delta\[1\] as 2Layer, ByThistypepush.
    -   frame\_num: SetEachoneLayerFrameNumber, where frame\_num \[0\] as 1Layer, frame\_num\[1\] as 2Layer, ByThistypepush.

-   LayerQPSchematic DiagramAs shown in [Figure 1](#fig152253434317)and[graph2](#fig16586145618411).

**Figure 1**  normal\_pLayerQPshown意graph<a name="fig152253434317"></a>  
![](figures/normal_p分层QP示意图.png "normal_p分层QP示意图")

**Figure 2**  smart\_p/adv\_smart\_ppLayerQPSchematic Diagram<a name="fig16586145618411"></a>  
![](figures/smart_p-adv_smart_pp分层QP示意图.png "smart_p-adv_smart_pp分层QP示意图")

**Example**

None.

**Related topics**

None.

-   This interface is used to set JPEGandMJPEGEncoding ChannelROIAttribute.
-   ROI parameters:
    -   idx: SupportedEachChannelCanSet16ROI, EncodingAccording to0~15IndexNo.toROIPerforms管arrange, idxIndicatesUserSetROIIndexNo..ROIBetweenCan互Overlay, AndWhenoccurOverlaywhen, ROIBetweenPriorityAccording toIndexNo.0~15SequentiallyHeight.
    -   enable: Specifies whether the current ROI is enabled.
    -   level: SpecifiedCurrentROIAdoptingreduceBitrateetc.Level, 0\~3SequentiallyHeight, etc.LevelHeightreduceBitratetoMore, ImageQualityLossalsotoMore.
    -   rect: Specifies the starting coordinates and size of the current ROI. ROI start coordinates must be within the image, 16-aligned; ROI width/height must be 16-aligned and greater than 0; ROI must be within the image.

-   This is an advanced API. ROI is not enabled by default; the user must call this API to enable it.
-   This interface is set after the encoding channel is created and before it is destroyed. When called during encoding, it takes effect on the next frame.
-   Non-ROIRegionleveldefaults to 2.
-   It is recommended to call this interface after creating the channel and before starting encoding to minimize calls during encoding.
-   It is recommended to call this interfaceBefore, first call [ss\_mpi\_venc\_get\_jpeg\_roi\_attr](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408098586)Interface, get current channelROIConfigure , before setting.

**Example**

None.

**Related topics**

None.

<tr id="row18541mcpsimp"><td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="50%" headers="mcps1.1.3.1.1 "><p id="p18543mcpsimp"><a name="p18543mcpsimp"></a><a name="p18543mcpsimp"></a>Non-0</p>
</td>
<td class="cellrowborder" valign="top" width="50%" headers="mcps1.1.3.1.2 "><p id="p18545mcpsimp"><a name="p18545mcpsimp"></a><a name="p18545mcpsimp"></a>On failure, see the error code.</p>
</td>
</tr>
</tbody>
</table>

**Requirements**

-   Header: ot\_comm\_venc.h, ss\_mpi\_venc.h
-   Library: libss\_mpi.a

**Note**

-   This API enables/disables the smart encoding function for H.264/H.265 encoding channels.
-   Smart encoding is supported under CBR, VBR, AVBR, QVBR, and CVBR rate control modes.
    -   In CBR mode, with the same target bit rate, enabling smart encoding enhances foreground image quality;
    -   In VBR, QVBR, or CVBR mode, enabling smart encoding can reduce bit rate while maintaining foreground quality, or improve foreground quality without changing background quality.
    -   In AVBR mode, the impact on bit rate after enabling depends on scene changes.

-   Smart encoding is mutually exclusive with QpMap mode, FixQp mode, ROI, PSkip, and MD-related APIs.

**Example**

None.

**Related topics**

None.

-   Header: ot\_comm\_venc.h, ss\_mpi\_venc.h
-   Library: libss\_mpi.a

**Note**

-   This interface must be called after the encoding channel is created and before it is destroyed.
-   For H.264 encoding channels, setting SPS and PPS parameter set IDs is supported. If two PPSs exist, the second PPS ID is the set value plus 1.
-   For H.265 encoding channels, setting VPS, SPS, and PPS parameter set IDs is supported.
-   This API is only supported for H.264/H.265 encoding channels.

**Example**

None.

**Related topics**

None.

-   This API is called after the encoding channel is created and before it is destroyed. If called during encoding, it takes effect at the next I-frame.
-   It is recommended to call this interface after creating the channel and before starting encoding to minimize calls during encoding.
-   It is recommended to call this interfaceBefore, first call [ss\_mpi\_venc\_get\_h264\_poc](#ZH-CN_TOPIC_0000002408258554)Interface, get current Encoding ChannelPOCConfigure Afteragainset.

At low bit rates, it may impact bit rate allocation and raise I-frame QP, affecting background quality. Balance background clarity against post-motion clarity; if background clarity is the priority, keep this API disabled.

**Requirements**

-   Header: ot\_common\_venc.h, ss\_mpi\_venc.h
-   Library: libss\_mpi.a

**Note**

-   This API sets the JPEG/MJPEG encoding channel ROI advanced attributes.
-   ROI parameters:
    -   idx: SupportedEachChannelCanSet16ROI, EncodingAccording to0~15IndexNo.toROIPerforms管arrange, idxIndicatesUserSetROIIndexNo..ROIBetweenCan互Overlay, AndWhenoccurOverlaywhen, ROIBetweenPriorityAccording toIndexNo.0~15SequentiallyHeight.
    -   enable: Specifies whether the current ROI is enabled.
    -   fg\_level: ROIRegionreduceBitrateetc.Level, 0\~15SequentiallyHeight, etc.LevelHeightreduceBitratetoMore, ImageQualityLossalsotoMore.
    -   bg\_level: Non-ROIRegionreduceBitrateetc.Level, 0\~15SequentiallyHeight, etc.LevelHeightreduceBitratetoMore, ImageQualityLossalsotoMore.
    -   rect: Specifies the starting coordinates and size of the current ROI. ROI start coordinates must be within the image, 16-aligned; ROI width/height must be 16-aligned and greater than 0; ROI must be within the image.
    -   Only supported4kindDifferentlevel, , i.e., at most3kindDifferentfg\_leveland1kindDifferentbg\_level.

-   This is an advanced API. ROI is not enabled by default; the user must call this API to enable it.
-   This interface is set after the encoding channel is created and before it is destroyed. When called during encoding, it takes effect on the next frame.
-   It is recommended to call this interface after creating the channel and before starting encoding to minimize calls during encoding.
